## Interpretation

Passing the mechanical checks proves that the proposed depth and length limits can represent every current file without omission, collision, or an overlength path. It does not prove that every heuristic subject placement is conceptually correct. Low-confidence placements and meaningful duplicates must be reviewed before migration.
